# 03 — Full Target Folder & File Structure (all-in layout)

This is the **complete** tree for the maximal version being evaluated: **every module in its own
folder** with its own `src/` and `tests/`, and **every layer as its own project**. It is generated by
mapping **every real file** in `src/` and `tests/` to its new home and injecting the new scaffolding
(`.csproj`, `Directory.*.props`, design-time factories, `.slnf` filters). The repository root keeps
exactly two code folders, `src/` and `tests/`, as today. **No `...` — every file is
listed.**

> **Names and test boundaries follow [stage 18](../implementation-specs/stage-18-project-restructure.md),
> which is the ruling document.** This tree no longer differs from stage 18's target tree at all: the one
> axis that remained — `Storage` and `Mailer` layer-split here, one project each there — closed when the
> owner ruled that every module is layered (D1). What follows is both the maximal shape and the shape
> being built.
>
> **There is no `<M>.Api` project.** An earlier draft split endpoints and MetaFields into one, and it
> was wrong on its own terms: the endpoint left the slice while its `*EndpointV1Tests.cs` stayed under
> `Application/`, so the vertical slice was shredded on the source side and not on the test side.
> Endpoints live in `<M>.Application` beside the command, handler and validator they serve — stage 18
> D1, and [02 §3](02-decision-b-layers-as-projects.md)'s "~586 files relocate to a Presentation
> project" is the cost that buys nothing.
>
> Everything else is stage 18's decision and matches it: `Shared.Domain` +
> `BuildingBlocks.{Domain,Application,Infrastructure,Presentation}` (D2, D3); single-module constants
> sent home to Identity and Storage (D4); `Core` renamed `Storage` (D7); per-module `<M>.TestData` /
> `<M>.Unit.Tests` / `<M>.Integration.Tests` (D8, D10); `tests/{TestData,Fixtures,EndToEnd.Tests,Architecture.Tests}`
> top-level and `Shared.Integration.Tests` under `src/shared/` (18.7).
>
> The recommendation is still **not** to go all the way to this — see
> [02-decision-b-layers-as-projects](02-decision-b-layers-as-projects.md),
> [05-testing-strategy](05-testing-strategy.md) and
> [07-migration-plan-and-verdict](07-migration-plan-and-verdict.md).

- **Regenerated from the live tree.** Every tracked file under `src/` and `tests/` plus the repository
  root files — **4,275 sources → 4,308 placed** across **38 projects** (15 `.csproj` retired, 48 new
  ones injected). Re-run the mapping rules below against `git ls-files` to reproduce it exactly.
- Mapping rules (deterministic, applied to every file):
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>/Domain/**` → `src/modules/<M>/<M>/src/<M>.Domain/**` — the module folder wraps `<M>/` (which carries its own `src/` and `tests/`) and `<M>.Contracts/` as peers — today's `src/Modules/<M>/{<M>, <M>.Contracts}` with `src/Modules/` dropped
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>/Application/**` → `src/modules/<M>/<M>/src/<M>.Application/**` — **endpoints included**: the 297 `V1/*EndpointV1.cs` and 297 `*MetaField.cs` stay in the slice next to their command, handler and validator
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>/Infrastructure/**` (incl. `Persistence`, `Migrations`, `Repositories`, `Services`) → `src/modules/<M>/<M>/src/<M>.Infrastructure/**`
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>/<M>Module.cs` → `src/modules/<M>/<M>/src/<M>.Infrastructure/<M>Module.cs`
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>.Contracts/**` → `src/modules/<M>/<M>.Contracts/**` — unchanged relative to today: the contracts project stays a **peer of the module's own project folder**, never one of its layers
  - `src/Shared/Shared/Domain/{Aggregate,Entity,IAggregate,IDomainEvent,IEntity,EnumAuditActor}.cs` → `src/shared/src/Shared.Domain/**` (zero package references)
  - `src/Shared/Shared/Domain/IRepository.cs` + `Application/Specifications/**` → `src/shared/src/BuildingBlocks.Domain/**`
  - `src/Shared/Shared.Contracts/Application/CQRS/**` + `Shared/Application/{Decorators,DTOs,Exceptions,Metadata,Pagination,Persistence,Services}/**` + `Application/Configurations/**` + `Builders/RateLimit/IAccountRateLimiter.cs` → `src/shared/src/BuildingBlocks.Application/**` (`Exceptions/Handlers/**` and the rest of `Builders/RateLimit/**` are presentation; `Jobs/` is infrastructure — all three measured in 18.2)
  - `src/Shared/Shared/Infrastructure/**` **except `Services/HttpCurrentActor.cs`** → `src/shared/src/BuildingBlocks.Infrastructure/**`
  - `src/Shared/Shared/Application/{Extensions,Middleware}/**` + `Application/Exceptions/Handlers/**` + `Builders/RateLimit/**` except `IAccountRateLimiter.cs` + `Infrastructure/Services/HttpCurrentActor.cs` + `BuildingBlocks/Constants/{Authorization,RateLimit}/**` + `Utils/ApiVersionUrl.cs` → `src/shared/src/BuildingBlocks.Presentation/**`
  - `BuildingBlocks/Constants/{Role,Session,Permission,JwtClaims}Constants.cs` and `UserConstants.cs` **minus the locale trio** → `src/modules/Identity/Identity/src/Identity.Domain/Constants/`
  - `UserConstants.{DefaultLocale,SupportedLocales,MaxLocaleLength}` → `src/shared/src/Shared.Domain/Constants/LocaleConstants.cs` — read by `LocalizationExtension`, Mailer, Content and Identity's own `UserEntity`, so they are **not** Identity's alone, and only the zero-package project is visible from a module's `Domain` (measured in 18.4)
  - `BuildingBlocks/Constants/FileConstants.cs` **minus the two video members** → `src/modules/Storage/Storage/src/Storage.Domain/Constants/`
  - `FileConstants.{MaxVideoFileSizeBytes,AllowedVideoExtensions,MaxAvatarFileSizeBytes,AllowedAvatarMimeTypes,AllowedAvatarExtensions}` → `src/modules/Storage/Storage.Contracts/Domain/Constants/FileUploadLimits.cs` — the video pair read by Content's `EditorialValidation` and the avatar trio by Identity's `FileValidation` ([02 §10](../02-module-boundaries.md) already said to expose these through Contracts; the avatar trio was measured in 18.6)
  - `src/Api/**` → `src/host/Api/**`
  - **Unit tests** `tests/Unit/Modules/<M>/**` → `src/modules/<M>/<M>/tests/<M>.Unit.Tests/**`
  - **Integration tests** `tests/Integration/Modules/<M>/**` → `src/modules/<M>/<M>/tests/<M>.Integration.Tests/**`
  - **Test data** `tests/Fixtures/**` and `tests/Unit/Common/Mocks/**` → `src/modules/<M>/<M>/tests/<M>.TestData/**` by owning module; the module-agnostic remainder (auth/http/culture helpers, shared constants, routes, `BaseHandlerTest`, `NoOpMigrator`) → `tests/TestData/**`. Namespace `_116.Tests.Fixtures` → `_116.Tests.TestData` in the same commit (stage 18 D10: the library holds builders and factories, not fixtures)
  - **Fixtures** `tests/Integration/Common/**` (`ApiFixture`, `PostgresFixture`, the Testcontainers, the xUnit collections, base classes, seeders, stubs) → `tests/Fixtures/**` — the `Common/Fixtures/` subfolder flattens into the project root, because this project *is* the fixtures
  - **Whole-app tests** `tests/Integration/{Workflows,Api}/**` + `SmokeTest.cs` + `GlobalUsings.cs` → `tests/EndToEnd.Tests/**`
  - **Shared-infrastructure integration tests** `tests/Integration/Shared/**` → `src/shared/tests/Shared.Integration.Tests/**`
  - `tests/Unit/{Shared,BuildingBlocks}/**` → `src/shared/tests/Shared.Unit.Tests/**`
  - `tests/Architecture/**` → `tests/Architecture.Tests/**`
- **Namespaces follow the project** ([stage 18](../implementation-specs/stage-18-project-restructure.md) D14):
  each module layer project sets `<RootNamespace>_116.<M></RootNamespace>`, so `_116.Content.Domain.*`,
  `_116.Content.Application.*` and `_116.Content.Infrastructure.*` are unchanged and **no `using` line moves**
  for the module split. The `src/shared/src` split is the opposite — `_116.Shared.{Application,Infrastructure,Contracts}`
  and `_116.BuildingBlocks.{Constants,Utils}` become `_116.BuildingBlocks.{Domain,Application,Infrastructure,Presentation}.*`,
  rewriting a `using` in 2,395 files. `_116.Shared.Domain` and `_116.Shared.Domain.Exceptions` stay put.
- Notes on the test projects, each of which stage 18 took a wrong turn on first:
  - `<M>.TestData`, `tests/TestData` and `tests/Fixtures` are **libraries, not test projects** — both
    the unit and the integration project of a module reference them, and a test project must never
    reference another test project.
  - `tests/Fixtures` is separate from `tests/TestData` because it drags Testcontainers, Respawn and
    `WebApplicationFactory`; only the five integration projects reference it, which keeps that graph
    out of every unit-test project.
  - `tests/EndToEnd.Tests` is **top-level, not under `src/host/`**: those flows reference fixtures from
    three different modules, so putting them under `src/host/` would make the deployable's folder depend
    sideways on four test libraries. `src/host/` stays a leaf.
  - **Mailer has no `Mailer.TestData`** — it has no builders or factories. The project appears when
    the first one does.
- `Core` → `Storage` renames every `Core*` / `ICore*` / `MockCore*` basename. Four names keep "Core" on
  purpose: the `20260111141504_InitCoreSchema` migration pair (migration filenames are historical record),
  `EnumCoreContentType` and `EnumCoreUserRole` (where "core" means primary, not the module).
- Notes on the source projects: `Storage` has **0 HTTP endpoints** (library module). `Content` has
  **no** `Content.Contracts` (not consumed by other modules). `Storage.Contracts` and the five
  `src/shared/src` projects are **new** (created during the move).

## Complete tree

> Split into collapsible sections so the 6,500-line listing can be read a piece at a time.
>
> **This is the all-in maximal shape the study rejected** (`07-migration-plan-and-verdict.md`), which the
> owner then adopted: every module layered. It is the shape being built, per
> [stage 18](../implementation-specs/stage-18-project-restructure.md), with **endpoints in
> `Application`** rather than in a `<M>.Api` project.
>
> `<M>.Contracts` sits at the **module root**, beside `src/` and `tests/` — a module's published
> seam is not one of its layers, and every other module references it without ever seeing `src/`.
>
> `Core` is shown as **`Storage`** per [13](../13-core-storage-and-settings-module.md) and stage 18
> D7. Three names deliberately keep "Core": `20260111141504_InitCoreSchema` (migration filenames
> are historical record and are never renamed), and `EnumCoreContentType` / `EnumCoreUserRole`,
> where "core" means primary, not the module. `Mailer` is **not** renamed here — `Notifications`
> ([14](../14-notifications-email-and-subscriptions.md)) has not been ruled on.
